## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```

```

```

```

```

  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter입니다. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 해볼 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を重視し、データが確実に届くよう再送や順序保証を行います。UDPは信頼性の仕組みを省いて軽く速く届けるため、再送や順序保証はありません。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de compartirla: poner tus commits al día sobre la rama principal o limpiar el historial. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la usan otras personas, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe el trabajo de quien los tenga.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、按顺序送达的字节流；UDP 则不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for a branch that's still yours, like local or unpushed work or a feature branch before its PR. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have based work on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/date.test.ts
```

只想跑名字匹配的几个测试，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）：

```bash
bun test -t "格式化日期"
```

两者可以组合，比如 `bun test src/utils/date.test.ts -t "格式化日期"`。

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records each change to a database before that change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes midway, it can replay the log on restart to recover committed work and discard anything that never finished.
```

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Board's ready: nights 2 and 3 are in, and each night's run will add its line.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2119 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">Not run yet</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Runs at 23:00 every night</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
  <script>
    function fmt(n) {
      return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k' : String(n);
    }
    function render(v) {
      var nights = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      var list = document.getElementById('list');
      list.textContent = '';
      if (!nights.length) {
        var empty = document.createElement('li');
        empty.className = 'k-empty';
        empty.textContent = 'Nightly results will list here.';
        list.appendChild(empty);
        document.getElementById('latest').textContent = 'Not run yet';
        document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'Runs at 23:00 every night';
        return;
      }
      nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
        var li = document.createElement('li');
        li.className = 'k-row';
        var main = document.createElement('div');
        main.className = 'k-row-main';
        main.textContent = 'Night ' + n.night;
        var sub = document.createElement('div');
        sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
        sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + (n.stalls == null ? '—' : n.stalls);
        var val = document.createElement('div');
        val.className = 'k-row-value';
        val.textContent = fmt(n.writes) + '/s';
        li.appendChild(main);
        li.appendChild(sub);
        li.appendChild(val);
        list.appendChild(li);
      });
      var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
      document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s';
      document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent =
        'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · night ' + last.night;
    }
    render(ducter.get('nights'));
    ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('nights')); });
    ducter.summary(fmt(0) === '0' ? '' : '');
  </script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

```

<style>
.hb-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.hb-main{flex:1;min-width:0}
.hb-streak{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted,#777);margin-top:4px}
.hb-strip{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(14,1fr);gap:4px;margin-top:12px}
.k-day{aspect-ratio:1;border-radius:5px;border:1px solid var(--d-line,#ddd);background:var(--d-sunken,#f3f3f0);color:var(--d-muted,#777);font-size:9px;padding:0;cursor:pointer}
.k-day[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--d-accent,#3a7d5c);border-color:var(--d-accent,#3a7d5c);color:#fff}
.k-day.today{outline:1px solid var(--d-ink,#222);outline-offset:1px}
.hb-foot{font-size:11px;color:var(--d-muted,#777);margin-top:6px}
</style>
<script>
const HABITS=ducter.get("habits") || [];
const DAY=86400000;
const app=document.getElementById('app');
let ticks={};
function pad(n){return n<10?'0'+n:''+n}
function keyOf(d){return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate())}
function today(){return keyOf(new Date())}
function dayNum(k){const p=k.split('-').map(Number);return Date.UTC(p[0],p[1]-1,p[2])/DAY}
function keyFromNum(n){const d=new Date(n*DAY);return d.getUTCFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getUTCMonth()+1)+'-' …  habit to tick today.')+'</div></section>';
  for(const h of HABITS){
    const done=on(h.id,t);
    html+='<section class="k-card"><div class="hb-row"><div class="hb-main">'
      +'<div class="k-h">'+h.name+'</div>'
      +'<div class="k-row-sub">'+h.sub+'</div>'
      +'<div class="hb-streak">Streak: <b>'+current(h.id)+'</b> day'+(current(h.id)===1?'':'s')+' · best '+best(h.id)+'</div>'
      +'</div><button class="k-btn'+(done?' primary':'')+'" data-h="'+h.id+'">'+(done?'Done ✓':'Tick today')+'</button></div>';
    html+='<div class="hb-strip">';
    for(let i=13;i>=0;i--){
      const k=keyFromNum(n-i);
      html+='<button class="k-day'+(i===0?' today':'')+'" aria-pressed="'+on(h.id,k)+'" data-h="'+h.id+'" data-day="'+k+'" title="'+k+'">'+Number(k.slice(-2))+'</button>';
    }
    html+='</div><div class="hb-foot">Last 14 days · tap any day to change it</div></section>';
  }
  app.innerHTML=html;
}
app.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('[data-h]');
  if(!b) return;
  toggle(b.dataset.h,b.dataset.day||today());
});
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('ticks')).then(v=>{ticks=v||{};render()});
}
ducter.onChange(load);
render();
load();

### app-edit-zh

```
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 加个合计，放在最上面，大一点
  [partner message] 好了，合计放在最上面、字放大了，现在三笔共 ¥1,966。
  [an app card under the message: changed "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2355 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  const total=document.getElementById("total");
  const count=document.getElementById("count");
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){
    total.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    count.textContent="记了的花销会加在这里";
    list.innerHTML='<div class="k-empty">还没记</div>';
    ducter.summary("还没记");
    return;
  }
  total.textContent="¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  count.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN"));
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent="¥"+Number(x.yuan).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    row.append(main,val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||!(yuan>0))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what,yuan,day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  });
  $('empty').hidden = list.length > 0;

  const sel = feeds.find(f => f.id === selected && f.date === t);
  $('del').hidden = !sel;
  if (sel) $('delText').textContent = sel.time + ' 喝了 ' + sel.ml + ' ml';
}

document.querySelectorAll('#chips .k-chip').forEach(b => {
  b.onclick = () => {
    $('ml').value = b.dataset.ml;
    document.querySelectorAll('#chips .k-chip').forEach(x => x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', x === b ? 'true' : 'false'));
  };
});

$('save').onclick = () => {
  const ml = Number($('ml').value);
  if (!isFinite(ml) || ml <= 0) { $('ml').focus(); return; }
  feeds.push({
    id: Date.now().toString(36),
    date: todayStr(),
    time: $('hh').value + ':' + $('mm').value,
    ml: ml
  });
  ducter.set(KEY, feeds);
  $('ml').value = '';
  document.querySelectorAll('#chips .k-chip').forEach(x => x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'));
  fillTimeSelects();
  $('save').closest('details').open = false;
  render();
};

$('delBtn').onclick = () => {
  feeds = feeds.filter(f => f.id !== selected);
  selected = null;
  ducter.set(KEY, feeds);
  render();
};

ducter.onChange(() => { load(); render(); });

load();
fillTimeSelects();
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"seed":[{"id":"seed1","date":"2026-11-24","time":"06:10","ml":120,"from":true},{"id":"seed2","date":"2026-11-24","time":"09:05","ml":90,"from":true}],"feeds":[{"id":"seed1","date":"2026-11-24","time":"06:10","ml":120,"from":true},{"id":"seed2","date":"2026-11-24","time":"09:05","ml":90,"from":true}]}

  function save() {
    if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("prs", prs);
  }

  if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(v => {
      if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) prs = v;
      render();
    });
  } else {
    render();

  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) {

      Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(v => {
        if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) { prs = v; render(); }
      });
    });
  }

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让改 TTL 测试的那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在 ~/dev/foo 的同一分支（ducter/foo-ttl）上再加一条 TTL 测试，覆盖“正好到期那一刻”的边界：
- 用已有的假时钟，把时间推进到恰好等于 TTL 的那个时刻，断言该值已经过期（或按现有约定断言边界行为，并在报告里说明你采用的约定）。
- 再补一个紧挨着边界前一刻（比到期少 1 毫秒或一个最小单位）的断言，确认仍然有效。
- 不要改动已有测试的意图，不要换回真实计时。
- 完成标准：新测试通过；把运行结果（包括连续多次运行的结果）和边界约定写进报告。
- 范围外：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。修改在本任务分支上提交即可。
```
